	scope:new_title = {
		#set_landless_title = yes
		#set_no_automatic_claims = yes
		#set_can_be_named_after_dynasty = no
		set_variable = store
		save_scope_as = store_title
	}

enact_spell_cost_effect = {
    subtract_quint = { AMOUNT = quint_cost  }
}

save_spell_effect = {
	save_scope_as = caster
	create_store_title = { NAME = GET_ROTE_NAME }
	scope:store_title = {
		save_scope_as = spell
		scope:caster = {
			every_in_list = {
				variable = spell_targets
				save_scope_as = item
				scope:spell = {
					add_to_variable_list = { name = spell_targets target = scope:item }
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { name = duration value = scope:caster.var:duration }
		set_variable = { name = duration_type value = scope:caster.var:duration_type }
		set_variable = { name = caster value = scope:caster }
		set_variable = { name = spell_difficulty value = scope:caster.spell_difficulty }
		set_variable = { name = current_successes value = 0 }
	}
	scope:spell = {
		create_store_title = { NAME = GET_ROTE_NAME }
		set_variable = { name = spell_name value = scope:store_title }
	}
	remove_variable = rote_name
	remove_localized_text = rote_name
	clear_variable_list = spell_targets
	add_to_variable_list = { name = casting_spells target = scope:spell }
}

enact_spell_effect = {
	scope:spell = {
		every_in_list = {
			variable = spell_targets
			limit = {
				has_variable_list  = true_magick_temp_mods
			}
			save_scope_as = target_title
			set_variable = { name = spell_name value = scope:spell.var:spell_name }
			var:target = {
				save_scope_as = target
				add_to_variable_list = {
					name = spells_on_character
					target = scope:target_title
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						scope:spell = {
							var:duration_type = flag:months
						}
					}
					trigger_event = { id = true_magick.3000 months = duration_from_spell }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						scope:spell = {
							var:duration_type = flag:years
						}
					}
					trigger_event = { id = true_magick.3000 years = duration_from_spell }
				}
				
			# }
			# true_magick_give_modifiers_effect = yes
			# true_magick_give_traits_effect = yes
			# var:target = {
				# hidden_effect = {
					# force_character_skill_recalculation = yes
				# }
			# }
			# if = {
				# limit = {
					# scope:spell = {
						# var:duration_type = flag:months
					# }
				# }
				
				# set_variable = { name = time_counter months = duration_from_spell }
			# }
			# else_if = {
				# limit = {
					# scope:spell = {
						# var:duration_type = flag:years
					# }
				# }
				# set_variable = { name = time_counter years = duration_from_spell }
			# }
			
		# }
	# }
	# #create_store_title = { NAME = GET_ROTE_NAME }
	# #set_variable = {name = spell value = scope:store_title}

# }
